*Model) appendItems(items []gh.Item) {\n\tif len(items) == 0 || m.project == nil {\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\tm.project.Items = append(m.project.Items, items...)\n\n\tknownOption := make(map[string]int, len(m.columns))\n\tnoStatusIdx := -1\n\tfor i, col := range m.columns {\n\t\tif col.optionID == noStatusOptionID {\n\t\t\tnoStatusIdx = i\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tknownOption[col.optionID] = i\n\t}\n\n\tknown := make(map[string]struct{}, len(m.project.Status.Options))\n\tfor _, opt := range m.project.Status.Options {\n\t\tknown[opt.ID] = struct{}{}\n\t}\n\n\tfor _, item := range items {\n\t\tif idx, ok := knownOption[item.StatusOptionID]; ok && item.StatusOptionID != noStatusOptionID {\n\t\t\tm.columns[idx].items = append(m.columns[idx].items, item)\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\t// item belongs to \"No Status\" — either it has no value, or its option\n\t\t// is not declared on the project (deleted). Lazily create the column.\n\t\tif noStatusIdx == -1 {\n\t\t\tm.columns = append(m.columns, column{optionID: noStatusOptionID, name: \"No Status\"})\n\t\t\tnoStatusIdx = len(m.columns) - 1\n\t\t}\n\t\tm.columns[noStatusIdx].items = append(m.columns[noStatusIdx].items, item)\n\t}\n\n\tm.loadedItems += len(items)\n}\n\nfunc buildColumns(p *gh.Project) []column {\n\tif p == nil {\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\tcols := make([]column, 0, len(p.Status.Options)+1)\n\tfor _, opt := range p.Status.Options {\n\t\tcols = append(cols, column{optionID: opt.ID, name: opt.Name})\n\t}\n\n\thasNoStatus := false\n\tfor _, item := range p.Items {\n\t\tplaced := false\n\t\tfor i := range cols {\n\t\t\tif cols[i].optionID == item.StatusOptionID && item.StatusOptionID != noStatusOptionID {\n\t\t\t\tcols[i].items = append(cols[i].items, item)\n\t\t\t\tplaced = true\n\t\t\t\tbreak\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t\tif !placed {\n\t\t\thasNoStatus = true\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\tif hasNoStatus {\n\t\tcols = append(cols, column{optionID: noStatusOptionID, name: \"No Status\"})\n\t\tfor _, item := range p.Items {\n\t\t\tbelongs := item.StatusOptionID == noStatusOptionID\n\t\t\tif !belongs {\n\t\t\t\tknown := false\n\t\t\t\tfor _, opt := range p.Status.Options {\n\t\t\t\t\tif opt.ID == item.StatusOptionID {\n\t\t\t\t\t\tknown = true\n\t\t\t\t\t\tbreak\n\t\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\tbelongs = !known\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tif belongs {\n\t\t\t\tidx := len(cols) - 1\n\t\t\t\tcols[idx].items = append(cols[idx].items, item)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn cols\n}\n\nfunc (m *Model) leftCol() int {\n\tif len(m.columns) == 0 {\n\t\treturn 0\n\t}\n\tif m.focusCol-1 < 0 {\n\t\treturn len(m.columns) - 1\n\t}\n\treturn m.focusCol - 1\n}\n\nfunc (m *Model) rightCol() int {\n\tif len(m.columns) == 0 {\n\t\treturn 0\n\t}\n\tif m.focusCol+1 >= len(m.columns) {\n\t\treturn 0\n\t}\n\treturn m.focusCol + 1\n}\n\nfunc (m *Model) currentItem() *gh.Item {\n\tif len(m.columns) == 0 {\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\tcol := m.columns[m.focusCol]\n\tif len(col.items) == 0 {\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\treturn &col.items[col.cursor]\n}\n==== internal/tui/update.go ====\npackage tui\n\nimport (\n\t\"errors\"\n\t\"fmt\"\n\t\"time\"\n\n\t\"github.com/atotto/clipboard\"\n\ttea \"github.com/charmbracelet/bubbletea\"\n\t\"github.com/pkg/browser\"\n\t\"github.com/shuntaka9576/kanban/internal/gh\"\n)\n\ntype bootstrapMsg struct {\n\tproject    *gh.Project\n\tnextCursor string\n\ttotalItems int\n\terr        error\n}\n\ntype itemsPageMsg struct {\n\titems      []gh.Item\n\tnextCursor string\n\ttotalItems int\n\terr        error\n}\n\ntype itemMovedMsg struct {\n\titemID string\n\terr    error\n}\n\ntype itemYankedMsg struct {\n\titemID   string\n\ttitle    string\n\tcomments int\n\tcapped   bool\n\terr      error\n}\n\ntype tickMsg struct{}\n\ntype clearStatusMsg struct{}\n\nconst (\n\tspinnerInterval = 120 * time.Millisecond\n\tstatusLifetime  = 4 * time.Second\n)\n\nfunc tickCmd() tea.Cmd {\n\treturn tea.Tick(spinnerInterval, func(time.Time) tea.Msg { return tickMsg{} })\n}\n\nfunc clearStatusAfter(d time.Duration) tea.Cmd {\n\treturn tea.Tick(d, func(time.Time) tea.Msg { return clearStatusMsg{} })\n}\n\nvar errNoStatusField = errors.New(\"project has no Status SingleSelect field; gh-kanban requires it\")\n\nfunc bootstrapCmd(client *gh.Client, spec gh.ProjectSpec) tea.Cmd {\n\treturn func() tea.Msg {\n\t\tres, err := client.BootstrapBySpec(spec)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn bootstrapMsg{err: err}\n\t\t}\n\t\tif res.Project != nil && res.Project.Status.ID == \"\" {\n\t\t\treturn bootstrapMsg{err: errNoStatusField}\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn bootstrapMsg{\n\t\t\tproject:    res.Project,\n\t\t\tnextCursor: res.NextCursor,\n\t\t\ttotalItems: res.TotalItems,\n\t\t}\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc fetchItemsPageCmd(client *gh.Client, projectID, statusFieldID, cursor string) tea.Cmd {\n\treturn func() tea.Msg {\n\t\tpage, err := client.FetchItemsPage(projectID, statusFieldID, cursor)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn itemsPageMsg{err: err}\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn itemsPageMsg{\n\t\t\titems:      page.Items,\n\t\t\tnextCursor: page.NextCursor,\n\t\t\ttotalItems: page.TotalItems,\n\t\t}\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc (m Model) Init() tea.Cmd {\n\treturn tea.Batch(bootstrapCmd(m.client, m.spec), tickCmd())\n}\n\nfunc (m Model) Update(msg tea.Msg) (tea.Model, tea.Cmd) {\n\tswitch msg := msg.(type) {\n\tcase tea.WindowSizeMsg:\n\t\tm.width, m.height = msg.Width, msg.Height\n\t\treturn m, nil\n\n\tcase tickMsg:\n\t\tif !m.bootstrapped || m.paginating || m.yanking != \"\" {\n\t\t\tm.spinnerFrame++\n\t\t\treturn m, tickCmd()\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn m, nil\n\n\tcase bootstrapMsg:\n\t\tif msg.err != nil {\n\t\t\tm.err = msg.err\n\t\t\tm.bootstrapped = true // unblock keys; user can quit/refresh\n\t\t\tm.paginating = false\n\t\t\treturn m, nil\n\t\t}\n\t\tm.err = nil\n\t\tm.bootstrapped = true\n\t\tm.setProject(msg.project)\n\t\tm.nextCursor = msg.nextCursor\n\t\tm.totalItems = msg.totalItems\n\t\tif m.nextCursor != \"\" {\n\t\t\tm.paginating = true\n\t\t\treturn m, tea.Batch(\n\t\t\t\tfetchItemsPageCmd(m.client, m.project.ID, m.project.Status.ID, m.nextCursor),\n\t\t\t\ttickCmd(),\n\t\t\t)\n\t\t}\n\t\tm.paginating = false\n\t\treturn m, nil\n\n\tcase itemsPageMsg:\n\t\tif msg.err != nil {\n\t\t\tm.err = msg.err\n\t\t\tm.paginating = false\n\t\t\treturn m, nil\n\t\t}\n\t\tm.appendItems(msg.items)\n\t\tm.nextCursor = msg.nextCursor\n\t\tif msg.totalItems > 0 {\n\t\t\tm.totalItems = msg.totalItems\n\t\t}\n\t\tif m.nextCursor != \"\" && m.project != nil {\n\t\t\tm.paginating = true\n\t\t\treturn m, fetchItemsPageCmd(m.client, m.project.ID, m.project.Status.ID, m.nextCursor)\n\t\t}\n\t\tm.paginating = false\n\t\treturn m, nil\n\n\tcase itemMovedMsg:\n\t\tm.movingItem = \"\"\n\t\tif msg.err != nil {\n\t\t\tm.err = msg.err\n\t\t\treturn m, nil\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn m.refresh()\n\n\tcase itemYankedMsg:\n\t\tm.yanking = \"\"\n\t\tif msg.err != nil {\n\t\t\tm.err = msg.err\n\t\t\treturn m, clearStatusAfter(statusLifetime)\n\t\t}\n\t\tmore := \"\"\n\t\tif msg.capped {\n\t\t\tmore = \" (truncated to first 100)\"\n\t\t}\n\t\tm.status = fmt.Sprintf(\"✔ Copied %q (%d comments%s) as Markdown.\", msg.title, msg.comments, more)\n\t\treturn m, clearStatusAfter(statusLifetime)\n\n\tcase clearStatusMsg:\n\t\tm.status = \"\"\n\t\treturn m, nil\n\n\tcase tea.KeyMsg:\n\t\treturn m.handleKey(msg)\n\t}\n\treturn m, nil\n}\n\nfunc (m Model) handleKey(msg tea.KeyMsg) (tea.Model, tea.Cmd) {\n\tswitch msg.String() {\n\tcase \"q\", \"ctrl+c\":\n\t\treturn m, tea.Quit\n\tcase \"R\":\n\t\treturn m.refresh()\n\t}\n\n\tif !m.bootstrapped || len(m.columns) == 0 {\n\t\treturn m, nil\n\t}\n\n\tcol := &m.columns[m.focusCol]\n\n\tswitch msg.String() {\n\tcase \"h\", \"left\":\n\t\tm.focusCol = m.leftCol()\n\n\tcase \"l\", \"right\":\n\t\tm.focusCol = m.rightCol()\n\n\tcase \"j\", \"down\":\n\t\tif len(col.items) > 0 {\n\t\t\tcol.cursor = (col.cursor + 1) % len(col.items)\n\t\t}\n\n\tcase \"k\", \"up\":\n\t\tif len(col.items) > 0 {\n\t\t\tcol.cursor = (col.cursor - 1 + len(col.items)) % len(col.items)\n\t\t}\n\n\tcase \"n\":\n\t\treturn m.moveItem(m.rightCol())\n\n\tcase \"b\":\n\t\treturn m.moveItem(m.leftCol())\n\n\tcase \"O\":\n\t\tif m.project != nil && m.project.URL != \"\" {\n\t\t\t_ = browser.OpenURL(m.project.URL)\n\t\t}\n\n\tcase \"o\":\n\t\tif it := m.currentItem(); it != nil && it.URL != \"\" {\n\t\t\t_ = browser.OpenURL(it.URL)\n\t\t}\n\n\tcase \"y\":\n\t\treturn m.yankItem()\n\t}\n\treturn m, nil\n}\n\nfunc (m Model) moveItem(targetCol int) (tea.Model, tea.Cmd) {\n\tif m.project == nil || m.project.Status.ID == \"\" {\n\t\treturn m, nil\n\t}\n\tif targetCol == m.focusCol {\n\t\treturn m, nil\n\t}\n\ttarget := m.columns[targetCol]\n\tif target.optionID == noStatusOptionID {\n\t\treturn m, nil\n\t}\n\titem := m.currentItem()\n\tif item == nil {\n\t\treturn m, nil\n\t}\n\n\tprojectID := m.project.ID\n\tfieldID := m.project.Status.ID\n\titemID := item.ID\n\toptionID := target.optionID\n\tclient := m.client\n\n\tm.movingItem = itemID\n\n\tcmd := func() tea.Msg {\n\t\terr := client.UpdateItemStatus(projectID, itemID, fieldID, optionID)\n\t\treturn itemMovedMsg{itemID: itemID, err: err}\n\t}\n\treturn m, cmd\n}\n\nfunc (m Model) yankItem() (tea.Model, tea.Cmd) {\n\titem := m.currentItem()\n\tif item == nil {\n\t\treturn m, nil\n\t}\n\titemID := item.ID\n\tclient := m.client\n\n\tm.yanking = itemID\n\tm.status = \"Fetching item context...\"\n\n\tcmd := tea.Batch(\n\t\tfunc() tea.Msg {\n\t\t\tctx, err := client.FetchItemContext(itemID)\n\t\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\t\treturn itemYankedMsg{itemID: itemID, err: err}\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tmd := renderItemMarkdown(ctx)\n\t\t\tif err := clipboard.WriteAll(md); err != nil {\n\t\t\t\treturn itemYankedMsg{itemID: itemID, err: fmt.Errorf(\"clipboard: %w\", err)}\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\treturn itemYankedMsg{\n\t\t\t\titemID:   itemID,\n\t\t\t\ttitle:    ctx.Title,\n\t\t\t\tcomments: len(ctx.Comments),\n\t\t\t\tcapped:   ctx.CommentsCapped,\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t},\n\t\ttickCmd(),\n\t)\n\treturn m, cmd\n}\n\nfunc (m Model) refresh() (tea.Model, tea.Cmd) {\n\t// Wipe board state but keep spec/specLabel so the loading view shows.\n\tm.project = nil\n\tm.columns = nil\n\tm.focusCol = 0\n\tm.bootstrapped = false\n\tm.paginating = false\n\tm.nextCursor = \"\"\n\tm.loadedItems = 0\n\tm.totalItems = 0\n\tm.err = nil\n\treturn m, tea.Batch(bootstrapCmd(m.client, m.spec), tickCmd())\n}\n==== internal/tui/view_test.go ====\npackage tui\n\nimport gh.ProjectSpec, specLabel string) Model {\n\treturn Model{\n\t\tclient:    client,\n\t\tspec:      spec,\n\t\tspecLabel: specLabel,\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc (m *Model) setProject(p *gh.Project) {\n\tm.project = p\n\tm.columns = buildColumns(p)\n\tif m.focusCol >= len(m.columns) {\n\t\tm.focusCol = 0\n\t}\n\tif p == nil {\n\t\tm.loadedItems = 0\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\tm.loadedItems = len(p.Items)\n}\n\nfunc (m *Model) appendItems(items []gh.Item) {\n\tif len(items) == 0 || m.project == nil {\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\tm.project.Items = append(m.project.Items, items...)\n\n\tknownOption := make(map[string]int, len(m.columns))\n\tnoStatusIdx := -1\n\tfor i, col := range m.columns {\n\t\tif col.optionID == noStatusOptionID {\n\t\t\tnoStatusIdx = i\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tknownOption[col.optionID] = i\n\t}\n\n\tknown := make(map[string]struct{}, len(m.project.Status.Options))\n\tfor _, opt := range m.project.Status.Options {\n\t\tknown[opt.ID] = struct{}{}\n\t}\n\n\tfor _, item := range items {\n\t\tif idx, ok := knownOption[item.StatusOptionID]; ok && item.StatusOptionID != noStatusOptionID {\n\t\t\tm.columns[idx].items = append(m.columns[idx].items, item)\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\t// item belongs to \"No Status\" — either it has no value, or its option\n\t\t// is not declared on the project (deleted). Lazily create the column.\n\t\tif noStatusIdx == -1 {\n\t\t\tm.columns = append(m.columns, column{optionID: noStatusOptionID, name: \"No Status\"})\n\t\t\tnoStatusIdx = len(m.columns) - 1\n\t\t}\n\t\tm.columns[noStatusIdx].items = append(m.columns[noStatusIdx].items, item)\n\t}\n\n\tm.loadedItems += len(items)\n}\n\nfunc buildColumns(p *gh.Project) []column {\n\tif p == nil {\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\tcols := make([]column, 0, len(p.Status.Options)+1)\n\tfor _, opt := range p.Status.Options {\n\t\tcols = append(cols, column{optionID: opt.ID, name: opt.Name})\n\t}\n\n\thasNoStatus := false\n\tfor _, item := range p.Items {\n\t\tplaced := false\n\t\tfor i := range cols {\n\t\t\tif cols[i].optionID == item.StatusOptionID && item.StatusOptionID != noStatusOptionID {\n\t\t\t\tcols[i].items = append(cols[i].items, item)\n\t\t\t\tplaced = true\n\t\t\t\tbreak\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t\tif !placed {\n\t\t\thasNoStatus = true\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\tif hasNoStatus {\n\t\tcols = append(cols, column{optionID: noStatusOptionID, name: \"No Status\"})\n\t\tfor _, item := range p.Items {\n\t\t\tbelongs := item.StatusOptionID == noStatusOptionID\n\t\t\tif !belongs {\n\t\t\t\tknown := false\n\t\t\t\tfor _, opt := range p.Status.Options {\n\t\t\t\t\tif opt.ID == item.StatusOptionID {\n\t\t\t\t\t\tknown = true\n\t\t\t\t\t\tbreak\n\t\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\tbelongs = !known\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tif belongs {\n\t\t\t\tidx := len(cols) - 1\n\t\t\t\tcols[idx].items = append(cols[idx].items, item)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn cols\n}\n\nfunc (m *Model) leftCol() int {\n\tif len(m.columns) == 0 {\n\t\treturn 0\n\t}\n\tif m.focusCol-1 < 0 {\n\t\treturn len(m.columns) - 1\n\t}\n\treturn m.focusCol - 1\n}\n\nfunc (m *Model) rightCol() int {\n\tif len(m.columns) == 0 {\n\t\treturn 0\n\t}\n\tif m.focusCol+1 >= len(m.columns) {\n\t\treturn 0\n\t}\n\treturn m.focusCol + 1\n}\n\nfunc (m *Model) currentItem() *gh.Item {\n\tif len(m.columns) == 0 {\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\tcol := m.columns[m.focusCol]\n\tif len(col.items) == 0 {\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\treturn &col.items[col.cursor]\n}\n==== internal/tui/update.go ====\npackage tui\n\nimport (\n\t\"errors\"\n\t\"fmt\"\n\t\"time\"\n\n\t\"github.com/atotto/clipboard\"\n\ttea \"github.com/charmbracelet/bubbletea\"\n\t\"github.com/pkg/browser\"\n\t\"github.com/shuntaka9576/kanban/internal/gh\"\n)\n\ntype bootstrapMsg struct {\n\tproject    *gh.Project\n\tnextCursor string\n\ttotalItems int\n\terr        error\n}\n\ntype itemsPageMsg struct {\n\titems      []gh.Item\n\tnextCursor string\n\ttotalItems int\n\terr        error\n}\n\ntype itemMovedMsg struct {\n\titemID string\n\terr    error\n}\n\ntype itemYankedMsg struct {\n\titemID   string\n\ttitle    string\n\tcomments int\n\tcapped   bool\n\terr      error\n}\n\ntype tickMsg struct{}\n\ntype clearStatusMsg struct{}\n\nconst (\n\tspinnerInterval = 120 * time.Millisecond\n\tstatusLifetime  = 4 * time.Second\n)\n\nfunc tickCmd() tea.Cmd {\n\treturn tea.Tick(spinnerInterval, func(time.Time) tea.Msg { return tickMsg{} })\n}\n\nfunc clearStatusAfter(d time.Duration) tea.Cmd {\n\treturn tea.Tick(d, func(time.Time) tea.Msg { return clearStatusMsg{} })\n}\n\nvar errNoStatusField = errors.New(\"project has no Status SingleSelect field; gh-kanban requires it\")\n\nfunc bootstrapCmd(client *gh.Client, spec gh.ProjectSpec) tea.Cmd {\n\treturn func() tea.Msg {\n\t\tres, err := client.BootstrapBySpec(spec)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn bootstrapMsg{err: err}\n\t\t}\n\t\tif res.Project != nil && res.Project.Status.ID == \"\" {\n\t\t\treturn bootstrapMsg{err: errNoStatusField}\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn bootstrapMsg{\n\t\t\tproject:    res.Project,\n\t\t\tnextCursor: res.NextCursor,\n\t\t\ttotalItems: res.TotalItems,\n\t\t}\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc fetchItemsPageCmd(client *gh.Client, projectID, statusFieldID, cursor string) tea.Cmd {\n\treturn func() tea.Msg {\n\t\tpage, err := client.FetchItemsPage(projectID, statusFieldID, cursor)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn itemsPageMsg{err: err}\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn itemsPageMsg{\n\t\t\titems:      page.Items,\n\t\t\tnextCursor: page.NextCursor,\n\t\t\ttotalItems: page.TotalItems,\n\t\t}\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc (m Model) Init() tea.Cmd {\n\treturn tea.Batch(bootstrapCmd(m.client, m.spec), tickCmd())\n}\n\nfunc (m Model) Update(msg tea.Msg) (tea.Model, tea.Cmd) {\n\tswitch msg := msg.(type) {\n\tcase tea.WindowSizeMsg:\n\t\tm.width, m.height = msg.Width, msg.Height\n\t\treturn m, nil\n\n\tcase tickMsg:\n\t\tif !m.bootstrapped || m.paginating || m.yanking != \"\" {\n\t\t\tm.spinnerFrame++\n\t\t\treturn m, tickCmd()\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn m, nil\n\n\tcase bootstrapMsg:\n\t\tif msg.err != nil {\n\t\t\tm.err = msg.err\n\t\t\tm.bootstrapped = true // unblock keys; user can quit/refresh\n\t\t\tm.paginating = false\n\t\t\treturn m, nil\n\t\t}\n\t\tm.err = nil\n\t\tm.bootstrapped = true\n\t\tm.setProject(msg.project)\n\t\tm.nextCursor = msg.nextCursor\n\t\tm.totalItems = msg.totalItems\n\t\tif m.nextCursor != \"\" {\n\t\t\tm.paginating = true\n\t\t\treturn m, tea.Batch(\n\t\t\t\tfetchItemsPageCmd(m.client, m.project.ID, m.project.Status.ID, m.nextCursor),\n\t\t\t\ttickCmd(),\n\t\t\t)\n\t\t}\n\t\tm.paginating = false\n\t\treturn m, nil\n\n\tcase itemsPageMsg:\n\t\tif msg.err != nil {\n\t\t\tm.err = msg.err\n\t\t\tm.paginating = false\n\t\t\treturn m, nil\n\t\t}\n\t\tm.appendItems(msg.items)\n\t\tm.nextCursor = msg.nextCursor\n\t\tif msg.totalItems > 0 {\n\t\t\tm.totalItems = msg.totalItems\n\t\t}\n\t\tif m.nextCursor != \"\" && m.project != nil {\n\t\t\tm.paginating = true\n\t\t\treturn m, fetchItemsPageCmd(m.client, m.project.ID, m.project.Status.ID, m.nextCursor)\n\t\t}\n\t\tm.paginating = false\n\t\treturn m, nil\n\n\tcase itemMovedMsg:\n\t\tm.movingItem = \"\"\n\t\tif msg.err != nil {\n\t\t\tm.err = msg.err\n\t\t\treturn m, nil\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn m.refresh()\n\n\tcase itemYankedMsg:\n\t\tm.yanking = \"\"\n\t\tif msg.err != nil {\n\t\t\tm.err = msg.err\n\t\t\treturn m, clearStatusAfter(statusLifetime)\n\t\t}\n\t\tmore := \"\"\n\t\tif msg.capped {\n\t\t\tmore = \" (truncated to first 100)\"\n\t\t}\n\t\tm.status = fmt.Sprintf(\"✔ Copied %q (%d comments%s) as Markdown.\", msg.title, msg.comments, more)\n\t\treturn m, clearStatusAfter(statusLifetime)\n\n\tcase clearStatusMsg:\n\t\tm.status = \"\"\n\t\treturn m, nil\n\n\tcase tea.KeyMsg:\n\t\treturn m.handleKey(msg)\n\t}\n\treturn m, nil\n}\n\nfunc (m Model) handleKey(msg tea.KeyMsg) (tea.Model, tea.Cmd) {\n\tswitch msg.String() {\n\tcase \"q\", \"ctrl+c\":\n\t\treturn m, tea.Quit\n\tcase \"R\":\n\t\treturn m.refresh()\n\t}\n\n\tif !m.bootstrapped || len(m.columns) == 0 {\n\t\treturn m, nil\n\t}\n\n\tcol := &m.columns[m.focusCol]\n\n\tswitch msg.String() {\n\tcase \"h\", \"left\":\n\t\tm.focusCol = m.leftCol()\n\n\tcase \"l\", \"right\":\n\t\tm.focusCol = m.rightCol()\n\n\tcase \"j\", \"down\":\n\t\tif len(col.items) > 0 {\n\t\t\tcol.cursor = (col.cursor + 1) % len(col.items)\n\t\t}\n\n\tcase \"k\", \"up\":\n\t\tif len(col.items) > 0 {\n\t\t\tcol.cursor = (col.cursor - 1 + len(col.items)) % len(col.items)\n\t\t}\n\n\tcase \"n\":\n\t\treturn m.moveItem(m.rightCol())\n\n\tcase \"b\":\n\t\treturn m.moveItem(m.leftCol())\n\n\tcase \"O\":\n\t\tif m.project != nil && m.project.URL != \"\" {\n\t\t\t_ = browser.OpenURL(m.project.URL)\n\t\t}\n\n\tcase \"o\":\n\t\tif it := m.currentItem(); it != nil && it.URL != \"\" {\n\t\t\t_ = browser.OpenURL(it.URL)\n\t\t}\n\n\tcase \"y\":\n\t\treturn m.yankItem()\n\t}\n\treturn m, nil\n}\n\nfunc (m Model) moveItem(targetCol int) (tea.Model, tea.Cmd) {\n\tif m.project == nil || m.project.Status.ID == \"\" {\n\t\treturn m, nil\n\t}\n\tif targetCol == m.focusCol {\n\t\treturn m, nil\n\t}\n\ttarget := m.columns[targetCol]\n\tif target.optionID == noStatusOptionID {\n\t\treturn m, nil\n\t}\n\titem := m.currentItem()\n\tif item == nil {\n\t\treturn m, nil\n\t}\n\n\tprojectID := m.project.ID\n\tfieldID := m.project.Status.ID\n\titemID := item.ID\n\toptionID := target.optionID\n\tclient := m.client\n\n\tm.movingItem = itemID\n\n\tcmd := func() tea.Msg {\n\t\terr := client.UpdateItemStatus(projectID, itemID, fieldID, optionID)\n\t\treturn itemMovedMsg{itemID: itemID, err: err}\n\t}\n\treturn m, cmd\n}\n\nfunc (m Model) yankItem() (tea.Model, tea.Cmd) {\n\titem := m.currentItem()\n\tif item == nil {\n\t\treturn m, nil\n\t}\n\titemID := item.ID\n\tclient := m.client\n\n\tm.yanking = itemID\n\tm.status = \"Fetching item context...\"\n\n\tcmd := tea.Batch(\n\t\tfunc() tea.Msg {\n\t\t\tctx, err := client.FetchItemContext(itemID)\n\t\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\t\treturn itemYankedMsg{itemID: itemID, err: err}\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tmd := renderItemMarkdown(ctx)\n\t\t\tif err := clipboard.WriteAll(md); err != nil {\n\t\t\t\treturn itemYankedMsg{itemID: itemID, err: fmt.Errorf(\"clipboard: %w\", err)}\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\treturn itemYankedMsg{\n\t\t\t\titemID:   itemID,\n\t\t\t\ttitle:    ctx.Title,\n\t\t\t\tcomments: len(ctx.Comments),\n\t\t\t\tcapped:   ctx.CommentsCapped,\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t},\n\t\ttickCmd(),\n\t)\n\treturn m, cmd\n}\n\nfunc (m Model) refresh() (tea.Model, tea.Cmd) {\n\t// Wipe board state but keep spec/specLabel so the loading view shows.\n\tm.project = nil\n\tm.columns = nil\n\tm.focusCol = 0\n\tm.bootstrapped = false\n\tm.paginating = false\n\tm.nextCursor = \"\"\n\tm.loadedItems = 0\n\tm.totalItems = 0\n\tm.err = nil\n\treturn m, tea.Batch(bootstrapCmd(m.client, m.spec), tickCmd())\n}\n==== internal/tui/view_test.go ====\npackage tui\n\nimport nil\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn m.refresh()\n\n\tcase itemYankedMsg:\n\t\tm.yanking = \"\"\n\t\tif msg.err != nil {\n\t\t\tm.err = msg.err\n\t\t\treturn m, clearStatusAfter(statusLifetime)\n\t\t}\n\t\tmore := handleKey(msg tea.KeyMsg) (tea.Model, tea.Cmd) {\n\tswitch msg.String() {\n\tcase \"q\", \"ctrl+c\":\n\t\treturn m, tea.Quit\n\tcase \"R\":\n\t\treturn m.refresh()\n\t}\n\n\tif !m.bootstrapped || len(m.columns) == 0 {\n\t\treturn m, nil\n\t}\n\n\tcol := &m.columns[m.focusCol]\n\n\tswitch msg.String() {\n\tcase \"h\", \"left\":\n\t\tm.focusCol = m.leftCol()\n\n\tcase \"l\", \"right\":\n\t\tm.focusCol = m.rightCol()\n\n\tcase \"j\", \"down\":\n\t\tif len(col.items) > 0 {\n\t\t\tcol.cursor = (col.cursor + 1) % len(col.items)\n\t\t}\n\n\tcase \"k\", \"up\":\n\t\tif len(col.items) > 0 {\n\t\t\tcol.cursor = (col.cursor - 1 + len(col.items)) % len(col.items)\n\t\t}\n\n\tcase \"n\":\n\t\treturn m.moveItem(m.rightCol())\n\n\tcase \"b\":\n\t\treturn m.moveItem(m.leftCol())\n\n\tcase \"O\":\n\t\tif m.project != nil && m.project.URL != \"\" {\n\t\t\t_ = browser.OpenURL(m.project.URL)\n\t\t}\n\n\tcase \"o\":\n\t\tif it := m.currentItem(); it != nil && it.URL != \"\" {\n\t\t\t_ = browser.OpenURL(it.URL)\n\t\t}\n\n\tcase \"y\":\n\t\treturn m.yankItem()\n\t}\n\treturn m, nil\n}\n\nfunc (m Model) moveItem(targetCol int) (tea.Model, tea.Cmd) {\n\tif m.project == nil || m.project.Status.ID == \"\" {\n\t\treturn m, nil\n\t}\n\tif targetCol == m.focusCol {\n\t\treturn m, nil\n\t}\n\ttarget := m.columns[targetCol]\n\tif target.optionID == noStatusOptionID {\n\t\treturn m, nil\n\t}\n\titem := m.currentItem()\n\tif item == nil {\n\t\treturn m, nil\n\t}\n\n\tprojectID := m.project.ID\n\tfieldID := m.project.Status.ID\n\titemID := item.ID\n\toptionID := target.optionID\n\tclient := m.client\n\n\tm.movingItem = itemID\n\n\tcmd := func() tea.Msg {\n\t\terr := client.UpdateItemStatus(projectID, itemID, fieldID, optionID)\n\t\treturn itemMovedMsg{itemID: itemID, err: err}\n\t}\n\treturn m, cmd\n}\n\nfunc (m Model) yankItem() (tea.Model, tea.Cmd) {\n\titem := m.currentItem()\n\tif item == nil {\n\t\treturn m, nil\n\t}\n\titemID := item.ID\n\tclient := m.client\n\n\tm.yanking = itemID\n\tm.status = \"Fetching item context...\"\n\n\tcmd := tea.Batch(\n\t\tfunc() tea.Msg {\n\t\t\tctx, err := client.FetchItemContext(itemID)\n\t\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\t\treturn itemYankedMsg{itemID: itemID, err: err}\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tmd := renderItemMarkdown(ctx)\n\t\t\tif err := clipboard.WriteAll(md); err != nil {\n\t\t\t\treturn itemYankedMsg{itemID: itemID, err: fmt.Errorf(\"clipboard: %w\", err)}\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\treturn itemYankedMsg{\n\t\t\t\titemID:   itemID,\n\t\t\t\ttitle:    ctx.Title,\n\t\t\t\tcomments: len(ctx.Comments),\n\t\t\t\tcapped:   ctx.CommentsCapped,\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t},\n\t\ttickCmd(),\n\t)\n\treturn m, cmd\n}\n\nfunc (m Model) refresh() (tea.Model, tea.Cmd) {\n\t// Wipe board state but keep spec/specLabel so the loading view shows.\n\tm.project = nil\n\tm.columns = nil\n\tm.focusCol = 0\n\tm.bootstrapped = false\n\tm.paginating = false\n\tm.nextCursor = \"\"\n\tm.loadedItems = 0\n\tm.totalItems = 0\n\tm.err = nil\n\treturn m, tea.Batch(bootstrapCmd(m.client, m.spec), tickCmd())\n}\n","numLines":309,"startLine":1,"totalLines":309}}}

For a tour of all the exciting new features, check out the [What's New](https://github.com/charmbracelet/bubbletea/releases/tag/v2.0.0) doc.\n\n> [!NOTE]\n> We don't take API changes lightly and strive to make the upgrade process as simple as possible. If something feels way off, let us know.\n\n## Migration Checklist\n\nHere's the short version — a checklist you can follow top to bottom. Each item links to the relevant section below.\n\n- [ ] [Update import paths](#import-paths)\n- [ ] [Change `View() string` to `View() tea.View`](#view-returns-a-teaview-now)\n- [ ] [Replace `tea.KeyMsg` with `tea.KeyPressMsg`](#key-messages)\n- [ ] [Update key fields: `msg.Type` / `msg.Runes` / `msg.Alt`](#key-messages)\n- [ ] [Replace `case \" \":` with `case \"space\":`](#key-messages)\n- [ ] [Update mouse message usage](#mouse-messages)\n- [ ] [Rename mouse button constants](#mouse-messages)\n- [ ] [Remove old program options → use View fields](#removed-program-options)\n- [ ] [Remove imperative commands → use View fields](#removed-commands)\n- [ ] [Remove old program methods](#removed-program-methods)\n- [ ] [Rename `tea.WindowSize()` → `tea.RequestWindowSize`](#renamed-apis)\n- [ ] [Replace `tea.Sequentially(...)` → `tea.Sequence(...)`](#renamed-apis)\n\n## Import Paths\n\nThe module path changed to a vanity domain. Lip Gloss moved too.\n\n```go\n// Before\nimport tea \"github.com/charmbracelet/bubbletea\"\nimport \"github.com/charmbracelet/lipgloss\"\n\n// After\nimport tea \"charm.land/bubbletea/v2\"\nimport \"charm.land/lipgloss/v2\"\n```\n\n## The Big Idea: Declarative Views\n\nThe single biggest change in v2 is the shift from **imperative commands** to **declarative View fields**. In v1, you'd use program options like `tea.WithAltScreen()` and commands like `tea.EnterAltScreen` to toggle terminal features on and off. In v2, you just set fields on the `tea.View` struct in your `View()` method and Bubble Tea handles the rest.\n\nThis means: no more startup option flags, no more toggle commands, no more fighting over state. Just declare what you want and Bubble Tea will make it so.\n\n```go\n// v1: imperative — scattered across NewProgram, Init, and Update\np := tea.NewProgram(model{}, tea.WithAltScreen(), tea.WithMouseCellMotion())\n\n// v2: declarative — everything lives in View()\nfunc (m model) View() tea.View {\n    v := tea.NewView(\"Hello!\")\n    v.AltScreen = true\n    v.MouseMode = tea.MouseModeCellMotion\n    return v\n}\n```\n\nKeep this in mind as you go through the rest of the guide — most of the \"removed\" things simply moved into View fields.\n\n## View Returns a `tea.View` Now\n\nThe `View()` method no longer returns a `string`. It returns a `tea.View` struct.\n\n```go\n// Before:\nfunc (m model) View() string {\n    return \"Hello, world!\"\n}\n\n// After:\nfunc (m model) View() tea.View {\n    return tea.NewView(\"Hello, world!\")\n}\n```\n\nYou can also use the longer form if you need to set additional fields:\n\n```go\nfunc (m model) View() tea.View {\n    var v tea.View\n    v.SetContent(\"Hello, world!\")\n    v.AltScreen = true\n    return v\n}\n```\n\nThe `tea.View` struct has fields for everything that used to be controlled by options and commands:\n\n| View Field | What It Does |\n|---|---|\n| `Content` | The rendered string (set via `SetContent()` or `NewView()`) |\n| `AltScreen` | Enter/exit the alternate screen buffer |\n| `MouseMode` | `MouseModeNone`, `MouseModeCellMotion`, or `MouseModeAllMotion` |\n| `ReportFocus` | Enable focus/blur event reporting |\n| `DisableBracketedPasteMode` | Disable bracketed paste |\n| `WindowTitle` | Set the terminal window title |\n| `Cursor` | Control cursor position, shape, color, and blink |\n| `ForegroundColor` | Set the terminal foreground color |\n| `BackgroundColor` | Set the terminal background color |\n| `ProgressBar` | Show a native terminal progress bar |\n| `KeyboardEnhancements` | Request keyboard enhancement features |\n| `OnMouse` | Intercept mouse messages based on view content |\n\n## Key Messages\n\nKey messages got a major overhaul. Here's the quick rundown:\n\n### `tea.KeyMsg` is now an interface\n\nIn v1, `tea.KeyMsg` was a struct you'd match on for key presses. In v2, it's an **interface** that covers both key presses and releases. For most code, you want `tea.KeyPressMsg`:\n\n```go\n// Before:\ncase tea.KeyMsg:\n    switch msg.String() {\n    case \"q\":\n        return m, tea.Quit\n    }\n\n// After:\ncase tea.KeyPressMsg:\n    switch msg.String() {\n    case \"q\":\n        return m, tea.Quit\n    }\n```\n\nIf you want to handle both presses and releases, use `tea.KeyMsg` and type-switch inside:\n\n```go\ncase tea.KeyMsg:\n    switch key := msg.(type) {\n    case tea.KeyPressMsg:\n        // key press\n    case tea.KeyReleaseMsg:\n        // key release\n    }\n```\n\n### Key fields changed\n\n| v1 | v2 | Notes |\n|---|---|---|\n| `msg.Type` | `msg.Code` | A `rune` — can be `tea.KeyEnter`, `'a'`, etc. |\n| `msg.Runes` | `msg.Text` | Now a `string`, not `[]rune` |\n| `msg.Alt` | `msg.Mod` | `msg.Mod.Contains(tea.ModAlt)` for alt, etc. |\n| `tea.KeyRune` | — | Check `len(msg.Text) > 0` instead |\n| `tea.KeyCtrlC` | — | Use `msg.String() == \"ctrl+c\"` or check `msg.Code` + `msg.Mod` |\n\n### Space bar changed\n\nSpace bar now returns `\"space\"` instead of `\" \"` when using `msg.String()`:\n\n```go\n// Before:\ncase \" \":\n\n// After:\ncase \"space\":\n```\n\n`key.Code` is still `' '` and `key.Text` is still `\" \"`, but `String()` returns `\"space\"`.\n\n### Ctrl+key matching\n\n```go\n// Before:\ncase tea.KeyCtrlC:\n    // ctrl+c\n\n// After (option A — string matching):\ncase tea.KeyPressMsg:\n    switch msg.String() {\n    case \"ctrl+c\":\n        // ctrl+c\n    }\n\n// After (option B — field matching):\ncase tea.KeyPressMsg:\n    if msg.Code == 'c' && msg.Mod == tea.ModCtrl {\n        // ctrl+c\n    }\n```\n\n### New Key fields\n\nThese are new in v2 and don't have v1 equivalents:\n\n- **`key.ShiftedCode`** — the shifted key code (e.g., `'B'` when pressing shift+b)\n- **`key.BaseCode`** — the key on a US PC-101 layout (handy for international keyboards)\n- **`key.IsRepeat`** — whether the key is auto-repeating (Kitty protocol / Windows Console only)\n- **`key.Keystroke()`** — like `String()` but always includes modifier info\n\n## Paste Messages\n\nPaste events no longer come in as `tea.KeyMsg` with a `Paste` flag. They're now their own message types:\n\n```go\n// Before:\ncase tea.KeyMsg:","is_error":false}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"1759996f-bde7-45e8-944d-64215766ebce","uuid":"6c0f6475-2d21-4edc-88ea-d5353c64f7e7","timestamp":"2026-07-02T06:43:48.078Z","tool_use_result":{"stdout":"=== UPGRADE GUIDE ===\n# Bubble Tea v2 Upgrade Guide\n\nThis guide covers everything you need to change when upgrading from Bubble Tea v1 to v2. If something feels way off, let us know.\n\n## Migration Checklist\n\nHere's the short version — a checklist you can follow top to bottom. Each item links to the relevant section below.\n\n- [ ] [Update import paths](#import-paths)\n- [ ] [Change `View() string` to `View() tea.View`](#view-returns-a-teaview-now)\n- [ ] [Replace `tea.KeyMsg` with `tea.KeyPressMsg`](#key-messages)\n- [ ] [Update key fields: `msg.Type` / `msg.Runes` / `msg.Alt`](#key-messages)\n- [ ] [Replace `case \" \":` with `case \"space\":`](#key-messages)\n- [ ] [Update mouse message usage](#mouse-messages)\n- [ ] [Rename mouse button constants](#mouse-messages)\n- [ ] [Remove old program options → use View fields](#removed-program-options)\n- [ ] [Remove imperative commands → use View fields](#removed-commands)\n- [ ] [Remove old program methods](#removed-program-methods)\n- [ ] [Rename `tea.WindowSize()` → `tea.RequestWindowSize`](#renamed-apis)\n- [ ] [Replace `tea.Sequentially(...)` → `tea.Sequence(...)`](#renamed-apis)\n\n## Import Paths\n\nThe module path changed to a vanity domain. Lip Gloss moved too.\n\n```go\n// Before\nimport tea \"github.com/charmbracelet/bubbletea\"\nimport \"github.com/charmbracelet/lipgloss\"\n\n// After\nimport tea \"charm.land/bubbletea/v2\"\nimport \"charm.land/lipgloss/v2\"\n```\n\n## The Big Idea: Declarative Views\n\nThe single biggest change in v2 is the shift from **imperative commands** to **declarative View fields**. In v1, you'd use program options like `tea.WithAltScreen()` and commands like `tea.EnterAltScreen` to toggle terminal features on and off. In v2, you just set fields on the `tea.View` struct in your `View()` method and Bubble Tea handles the rest.\n\nThis means: no more startup option flags, no more toggle commands, no more fighting over state. Just declare what you want and Bubble Tea will make it so.\n\n```go\n// v1: imperative — scattered across NewProgram, Init, and Update\np := tea.NewProgram(model{}, tea.WithAltScreen(), tea.WithMouseCellMotion())\n\n// v2: declarative — everything lives in View()\nfunc (m model) View() tea.View {\n    v := tea.NewView(\"Hello!\")\n    v.AltScreen = true\n    v.MouseMode = tea.MouseModeCellMotion\n    return v\n}\n```\n\nKeep this in mind as you go through the rest of the guide — most of the \"removed\" things simply moved into View fields.\n\n## View Returns a `tea.View` Now\n\nThe `View()` method no longer returns a `string`. It returns a `tea.View` struct.\n\n```go\n// Before:\nfunc (m model) View() string {\n    return \"Hello, world!\"\n}\n\n// After:\nfunc (m model) View() tea.View {\n    return tea.NewView(\"Hello, world!\")\n}\n```\n\nYou can also use the longer form if you need to set additional fields:\n\n```go\nfunc (m model) View() tea.View {\n    var v tea.View\n    v.SetContent(\"Hello, world!\")\n    v.AltScreen = true\n    return v\n}\n```\n\nThe `tea.View` struct has fields for everything that used to be controlled by options and commands:\n\n| View Field | What It Does |\n|---|---|\n| `Content` | The rendered string (set via `SetContent()` or `NewView()`) |\n| `AltScreen` | Enter/exit the alternate screen buffer |\n| `MouseMode` | `MouseModeNone`, `MouseModeCellMotion`, or `MouseModeAllMotion` |\n| `ReportFocus` | Enable focus/blur event reporting |\n| `DisableBracketedPasteMode` | Disable bracketed paste |\n| `WindowTitle` | Set the terminal window title |\n| `Cursor` | Control cursor position, shape, color, and blink |\n| `ForegroundColor` | Set the terminal foreground color |\n| `BackgroundColor` | Set the terminal background color |\n| `ProgressBar` | Show a native terminal progress bar |\n| `KeyboardEnhancements` | Request keyboard enhancement features |\n| `OnMouse` | Intercept mouse messages based on view content |\n\n## Key Messages\n\nKey messages got a major overhaul. Here's the quick rundown:\n\n### `tea.KeyMsg` is now an interface\n\nIn v1, `tea.KeyMsg` was a struct you'd match on for key presses. In v2, it's an **interface** that covers both key presses and releases. For most code, you want `tea.KeyPressMsg`:\n\n```go\n// Before:\ncase tea.KeyMsg:\n    switch msg.String() {\n    case \"q\":\n        return m, tea.Quit\n    }\n\n// After:\ncase tea.KeyPressMsg:\n    switch msg.String() {\n    case \"q\":\n        return m, tea.Quit\n    }\n```\n\nIf you want to handle both presses and releases, use `tea.KeyMsg` and type-switch inside:\n\n```go\ncase tea.KeyMsg:\n    switch key := msg.(type) {\n    case tea.KeyPressMsg:\n        // key press\n    case tea.KeyReleaseMsg:\n        // key release\n    }\n```\n\n### Key fields changed\n\n| v1 | v2 | Notes |\n|---|---|---|\n| `msg.Type` | `msg.Code` | A `rune` — can be `tea.KeyEnter`, `'a'`, etc. |\n| `msg.Runes` | `msg.Text` | Now a `string`, not `[]rune` |\n| `msg.Alt` | `msg.Mod` | `msg.Mod.Contains(tea.ModAlt)` for alt, etc. |\n| `tea.KeyRune` | — | Check `len(msg.Text) > 0` instead |\n| `tea.KeyCtrlC` | — | Use `msg.String() == \"ctrl+c\"` or check `msg.Code` + `msg.Mod` |\n\n### Space bar changed\n\nSpace bar now returns `\"space\"` instead of `\" \"` when using `msg.String()`:\n\n```go\n// Before:\ncase \" \":\n\n// After:\ncase \"space\":\n```\n\n`key.Code` is still `' '` and `key.Text` is still `\" \"`, but `String()` returns `\"space\"`.\n\n### Ctrl+key matching\n\n```go\n// Before:\ncase tea.KeyCtrlC:\n    // ctrl+c\n\n// After (option A — string matching):\ncase tea.KeyPressMsg:\n    switch msg.String() {\n    case \"ctrl+c\":\n        // ctrl+c\n    }\n\n// After (option B — field matching):\ncase tea.KeyPressMsg:\n    if msg.Code == 'c' && msg.Mod == tea.ModCtrl {\n        // ctrl+c\n    }\n```\n\n### New Key fields\n\nThese are new in v2 and don't have v1 equivalents:\n\n- **`key.ShiftedCode`** — the shifted key code (e.g., `'B'` when pressing shift+b)\n- **`key.BaseCode`** — the key on a US PC-101 layout (handy for international keyboards)\n- **`key.IsRepeat`** — whether the key is auto-repeating (Kitty protocol / Windows Console only)\n- **`key.Keystroke()`** — like `String()` but always includes modifier info\n\n## Paste Messages\n\nPaste events no longer come in as `tea.KeyMsg` with a `Paste` flag. They're now their own message types:\n\n```go\n// Before:\ncase tea.KeyMsg:","stderr":"","interrupted":false,"isImage":false,"noOutputExpected":false}}
